        Note: Please see the introduction and background information
        provided in the file cathintr.txt.  See file cathla-l.txt for
        the remainder of the extraction.

        This indexing project was undertaken as a result of
        the start-up of the BERESAN DISTRICT ODESSA NEWSLETTER
        (BDO NEWSLETTER).

        AHSGR was approached about using their books in this manner
        and agreed that it was a worthy project to launch.  Mrs.
        Anna Smith heard of the project and took it on as a
        project she wanted to do.

        (Please - again, understand that the value/worth of this list is 
        limited unless you read and understand the file cathintr.txt.)

             References;
             1 - "The Emigration from Germany to Russia in 
                  the years 1763 to 1862" by Karl Stumpp
             2 - "The German Colonies In South Russia, 1804 - 1904", 
                  by Rev. Conrad Keller
